**AI Ethics, Bias & Fairness Runtime Monitor (EBFRM)** enforces **continuous ethical correctness** inside Salesforce—*at decision time*.

# **Enterprise AI Marketplace & Capability Exchange (EAMCX)**

> This product governs **DISTRIBUTION & REUSE**: a controlled, constitutional **marketplace** where AI capabilities (agents, prompts, policies, workflows, analytics packs) are **discovered, certified, monetized, deployed, and retired**—safely.

---

## 0️⃣ Executive truth (why EAMCX must exist)

Enterprises already have AI assets—**but no safe way to share them**.

What happens today:
- Teams re-build the same AI
- “Copy-paste intelligence” spreads risk
- Good ideas die in silos
- No economic signal for quality

**Enterprise AI Marketplace & Capability Exchange (EAMCX)** creates a **governed economy of intelligence**:
> Publish once → certify → reuse → measure → reward → retire.

This is not a plugin store.  
This is **capital markets for enterprise intelligence**.

---

## 1️⃣ Pain attacked (real bleeding, quantified)

| Area | Failure |
|---|---|
| Reuse | Same AI built 10× |
| Safety | Unreviewed reuse |
| Velocity | Slow discovery |
| Value | No monetization signal |
| Governance | No lifecycle control |

→ **£2–25M/year** (large orgs)

- Re-implementation  
→ **1,000–3,000 hours/year**

**🧠 Energy**
- “We already built this… somewhere”

### EAMCX is NOT

## 0️⃣ Executive truth (why EATCMI must exist)

Most AI programs fail for one reason:

> **Capability is uneven, invisible, and assumed.**

Reality today:
- A few experts carry the org
- Leadership overestimates readiness
- AI autonomy expands faster than skills
- Incidents are blamed on “the model”

**Enterprise AI Talent & Capability Maturity Index (EATCMI)** makes **human AI capability measurable, auditable, and improvable**—as a **system property**, not an HR opinion.

This is not training.  
This is **operational readiness for AI**.

---

## 1️⃣ Pain attacked (real bleeding, quantified)

| Area | Failure |
|---|---|
| Visibility | No true AI skill map |

| Scale | Heroes become bottlenecks |
| Governance | Policies ignored unintentionally |
| Trust | “People aren’t ready” fear |

### EATCMI is NOT
- A performance review
- A hiring tool

## 0️⃣ Executive truth (why EARCE must exist)

Enterprises plan for:

They **do not** plan for:

- Model hallucination cascade
- Emergency AI shutdown by Legal
- Ethics freeze mid-quarter
- Cost governor hard-stop

When AI fails without a continuity plan, **business stops**.

**Enterprise AI Resilience & Continuity Engine (EARCE)** makes AI:
> **Fail-safe, degradable, recoverable, and business-continuous.**

This is **BCP/DR for AI**.

# **Enterprise AI Sovereignty & Data Boundary Engine (AISDBE)**

## 0️⃣ Executive truth (why AISDBE must exist)

Enterprises say “our data is compliant.”  
Reality in AI era:
- Data silently crosses borders via models
- Vendors train on “temporary” inputs
- Agents recombine sensitive fields
- Jurisdictional rules conflict at runtime

**Enterprise AI Sovereignty & Data Boundary Engine (AISDBE)** enforces **hard, machine-enforced data sovereignty** for AI:
> **No prompt, agent, model, or workflow can access data it is not constitutionally allowed to access—anywhere, anytime.**

This is **data law as runtime control**, not policy PDFs.

---

## 1️⃣ Pain attacked (real bleeding, quantified)

- Board-level fear of GenAI

4. Prompt modified or blocked
5. Evidence preserved

2. AISDBE updates boundary rules
3. All affected AI calls instantly gated
4. No retroactive breach

## 0️⃣ Executive truth (why EALIE must exist)

When AI is challenged, the question is never *“Was the model good?”*  
It is always:

> **“Prove what happened, why it happened, who approved it, and that it complied with law and policy—at that moment.”**

Most enterprises cannot prove this **retroactively**.

**Enterprise AI Litigation & Evidence Engine (EALIE)** makes **every AI action litigation-ready by default**.

This is not e-discovery after the fact.  
This is **evidence-first AI operations**.

**Enterprise AI Insurance & Risk Transfer Engine (EAIRTE)** turns AI from an *unbounded risk* into a **priced, transferable liability**.

# 3) Data setup inside Salesforce (no local) — exact objects + sample data

## 3.1 Create 3 Custom Objects (Salesforce)
1) **AI_Component__c**  
Fields: Name, Type (Agent/Prompt/Workflow), OwnerTeam, Status

2) **AI_Execution_Event__c**  
Fields: Component (Lookup), EventTime, LatencyMs, Tokens, Cost, Result (Success/Fail), RiskScore

3) **AI_Policy_Check__c**  
Fields: Component (Lookup), PolicyName, CheckTime, Outcome (Pass/Fail), Severity, Notes

## 3.2 Create sample data (fast options)
Choose ONE:
- **Option A (UI):** Data Import Wizard → import 20–100 rows per object  
- **Option B (Apex Anonymous):** insert a small dataset in one run (repeatable)

**Output you should see:** records visible in Salesforce object tabs.

---

# 4) Semantic model structure (Tableau Next) — what to build
In Tableau Next:
- Create a **Semantic Model** over the three objects (or Data Cloud unified tables if you ingest there)
- Define relationships:
  - AI_Component__c (1) → AI_Execution_Event__c (many)
  - AI_Component__c (1) → AI_Policy_Check__c (many)

Create metrics (minimum 5):
1) **Execution Success Rate**
2) **Avg Latency**
3) **Total Cost**
4) **Policy Fail Count**
5) **High Severity Policy Fail Count**

This is exactly the “semantic model + metrics feed dashboards” concept Tableau Next is built for. citeturn0search8turn0search1

---

# 5) Dashboard architecture (Tableau Next) — exact layout
Create **1 dashboard** called: **Governance Command Center**

Widgets:
- KPI tiles: Success Rate, Cost, Policy Fail Count
- Trend line: Cost over time, Failures over time
- Table: Top risky components
- Filter: Component Type / OwnerTeam / Time range

**Output you should see:** interactive dashboard in Tableau Next.

---

  3) Update AI_Component__c Status = “Throttled”

Tableau Next shows “what’s happening”; Salesforce Flow **does the action**.
